echo; gh issue view 1 --json body --jq .body 2>&1 | head -150","description":"Read ticket and spec via JSON"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_01QPTmrfimGs6FxPwuAkSAoi":{"cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfWyXxYXkDyAKSaVY4aHW","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:implement","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"993dbd51-129d-4ab8-827f-ec9a6e3c1246","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:54:03.942Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"993dbd51-129d-4ab8-827f-ec9a6e3c1246","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c6fedfd3-69a3-44f3-8484-8f7e881e0baf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QPTmrfimGs6FxPwuAkSAoi","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nAn Editor can write a Post, save it as a Draft, edit it, and mark it Published. The Post gets its slug automatically. The permission rules exist in one central place, and an Editor who attempts an Admin-only action (deleting a Post, changing a slug) sees the \\\"You don't have permission\\\" page. Demonstrated with the demo Editor and Admin from T01.\\n\\n## Acceptance criteria\\n\\n- [ ] An Editor can create a Post with a title and a Markdown Body, save it as a Draft, edit it later, and mark it Published or unpublish it. The Author is recorded automatically as the signed-in account. Created, updated and First-published times are recorded automatically.\\n- [ ] The slug is generated from the title on first save, with a number added when it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or Body is later edited.\\n- [ ] The First-published date is set the first time the Post becomes Published and is kept if the Post is unpublished and Published again.\\n- [ ] An Editor can edit any Post, including one written by another account.\\n- [ ] Only an Admin can delete a Post, and only an Admin can change a slug. The slug form warns that changing it can break an old link.\\n- [ ] All permissions are checked by the server from one central rule table. Controls an Editor cannot use are hidden, but tests prove the server refuses the request when sent directly.\\n- [ ] An Editor who tries an Admin-only action, by typed address or direct submission, sees a clear \\\"You don't have permission\\\" page with a link back to the dashboard. A signed-out visitor is sent to sign-in instead.\\n- [ ] Every state-changing Post form carries a CSRF token.\\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover: Draft/Published transitions; slug generation, numbering and stability; First-published date rules; each allowed and refused case in the Post part of the permission matrix; the refusal page; and CSRF.\\n- [ ] The tests are read before they are allowed to pass, to catch a test written against a misunderstanding.\\n\\n## Blocked by\\n\\n- #2 (T01: blocking)\\n\\n\",\"comments\":[],\"number\":3,\"title\":\"T02: An Editor writes a Post, with roles and Permission refusal\"}\n\n## Problem Statement\n\nASA, the African Student Association at Kenyon, is described in the client brief as a student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. Students need an easy way to find event announcements (including an Afrobeat event), learn about the association, explore cultural resources, and see photos from past activities. Its leaders need event announcements to be quick to publish, information about ASA to stay easy to find in the navigation, and a way to share photos only when ASA has permission to use them. One ASA leader should manage accounts and permanent pages; another member should be able to write and update event posts without a complicated form.\n\nThis is a class project (IPHS 400, Mini-Project #2). No claim is made about any real ASA process, officers, schedule or history. Terms below are defined in `CONTEXT.md`.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS. A local admin console lets an Admin and Editors write **Posts** and **Pages**, manage accounts, and preview content. A `cms publish` step renders only **Published** content into a static public site, and `cms deploy` sends it to GitHub Pages, at which point the content is **Live**. The admin console never goes on the public internet (ADR-001).\n\nThe public site has a **Home page** (a short **Welcome text** taken from the client brief plus the five latest **Announcements**), an **Announcements** list, and **Navigation** built from published Pages (starter Pages for the demo: About ASA, Resources, Photos). Event announcements are ordinary Posts with the event's date, time, location and who can attend written in the **Body**. Photos are images inside Posts or Pages; there is no upload form.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Signing in\n1. As an Admin, I want to sign in with a username and password, so that only authorised people can change the site.\n2. As an Editor, I want to sign in and out, so that my session ends when I leave a shared computer.\n3. As an account owner, I want my password stored hashed, so that nobody, including the Admin, can read it.\n4. As an Admin, I want a wrong password to be rejected with a clear message that does not reveal whether the username exists, so that accounts cannot be guessed.\n5. As someone with a Deactivated account, I want sign-in to be refused, so that a former member can no longer change the site.\n6. As a signed-in user, I want every form I submit to be protected against forged requests, so that another website cannot act as me.\n7. As a visitor who is not signed in, I want any admin address to send me to sign-in, so that I never see admin content.\n\n### First Admin and setup\n8. As the project owner, I want the first Admin account created from the command line with a password taken from the environment, so that no password is committed to Git.\n9. As the project owner, I want a clean clone to run from `.env.example` plus a few documented steps, so that a grader can reproduce the demo.\n10. As the project owner, I want the database and `.env` kept out of Git, so that private data is never published.\n11. As the project owner, I want demo data that can be rebuilt on demand, so that screenshots and tests are repeatable.\n\n### Roles and permission refusal\n12. As an Admin, I want to manage accounts and permanent Pages, so that I control who can change the site and what the Navigation contains.\n13. As an Editor, I want to create and publish Posts directly, so that an announcement can go out quickly.\n14. As an Editor, I want to edit any Post, including another Editor's, so that event details can be corrected close to the event if the writer is unavailable.\n15. As an Editor, I want to update the Body of an existing Page, so that the Photos page can stay current.\n16. As an Admin, I want only Admins to delete Posts, so that a mistaken deletion is unlikely.\n17. As an Admin, I want only Admins to create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page, so that Navigation and links stay stable.\n18. As an Admin, I want only Admins to change a slug, and to be warned that doing so can break an old link, so that shared links keep working.\n19. As an Editor, I want admin-only controls hidden from my screens, so that my screens stay simple.\n20. As an Editor who reaches an Admin-only address or submits a forbidden action directly, I want a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard, so that I understand what happened.\n21. As an Admin, I want every permission enforced by the server, not by hidden buttons, so that access control is real.\n\n### Posts\n22. As an Editor, I want to write a Post with a title and a Body and save it as a Draft, so that I can finish it later.\n23. As an Editor, I want to mark a Post Published, so that it is ready for the public site.\n24. As an Editor, I want to unpublish a Post, so that a wrong announcement stops going out.\n25. As an Editor, I want the slug generated from my title on first save, so that I never have to think about URLs.\n26. As an Editor, I want a number added automatically when a slug already exists, so that two Posts never collide.\n27. As an Editor, I want the slug to stay unchanged when I correct the title or event details, so that shared links keep working.\n28. As an Editor, I want a short hint reminding me to include What, When, Where and Who can attend, so that announcements are complete.\n29. As an Editor, I want the hint not pre-filled into the Body and hideable without losing content, so that I can write naturally.\n30. As an Editor, I want the Post to record me as its Author automatically, so that I do not fill in extra fields.\n31. As an Editor, I want the Post's created, updated and first-published times recorded automatically, so that dates are trustworthy.\n\n### Pages\n32. As an Admin, I want to create a Page with a title, slug and Body, so that permanent information has a home.\n33. As an Admin, I want a published Page to appear in the public Navigation automatically, so that visitors can find it.\n34. As an Admin, I want an unpublished Page to disappear from the Navigation, so that unfinished pages are not linked.\n35. As an Admin, I want to delete a Page, so that obsolete pages can be removed.\n36. As an Editor, I want to edit the Body of a Page but see title, slug, status and delete controls as unavailable, so that I cannot break the Navigation.\n\n### Writing, preview and images\n37. As an Editor, I want to use headings, emphasis, lists, links and images with alt text, so that announcements read well without learning HTML.\n38. As an Editor, I want raw HTML, scripts, frames and forms in my text stripped, so that the site is safe.\n39. As an Editor, I want a preview that shows exactly what the public page will render, so that there are no surprises after publishing.\n40. As an Editor, I want to write a caption, alt text and Markdown reference for an image, so that photos are accessible.\n41. As an Editor, I understand I cannot add a new image file myself, so that the release-holder can confirm Photo permission first.\n42. As the release-holder, I want approved image files copied into the public site with relative paths, so that photos show up after deployment.\n43. As the release-holder, I want demo illustrations labelled as illustrative and never presented as photos of real ASA events, so that visitors are not misled.\n\n### Admin console\n44. As an Admin, I want a dashboard with the counts of Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages and Draft Pages, so that I see the state of the site at a glance.\n45. As an Admin, I want the dashboard to also show the number of active and Deactivated accounts, so that I can keep track of who has access.\n46. As an Editor, I want the dashboard to show the four content counts but no account counts, so that I only see what I need.\n47. As any signed-in user, I want a reminder on the dashboard that marking content Published does not make it Live until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run, so that I do not expect instant changes.\n48. As any signed-in user, I want one Content list I can filter by Post or Page and by Draft or Published, so that I can find content without opening each item.\n49. As any signed-in user, I want each row to show title, status, Display name and last-updated time, so that I can tell items apart.\n50. As a phone user, I want the admin console usable at phone width, so that I can post from anywhere.\n\n### User management\n51. As an Admin, I want to create an account with a username, a private password, a role and a public Display name, so that a new member can start writing.\n52. As an Admin, I want to change an account's role, so that responsibilities can change.\n53. As an Admin, I want to deactivate an account, so that a former member can no longer sign in while their published Posts stay.\n54. As an Admin, I want to reactivate a deactivated account, so that a mistake is recoverable.\n55. As an Admin, I want to set a new password for an existing account without seeing the old one, so that a lost password can be recovered.\n56. As an Admin, I want a reset password never printed or logged, so that it cannot leak.\n57. As an Admin, I want to change an account's Display name, so that a former member can ask for their name to be replaced with \"ASA Events Team\".\n58. As an Admin, I want the original Author link of every Post kept, even after a Display name change or deactivation, so that the record stays accurate.\n59. As an Admin, I want the private username never used as a byline by default, so that private account names do not appear publicly.\n60. As an Admin, I want accounts deactivated rather than deleted, so that history is preserved.\n\n### Public site\n61. As a first-time visitor, I want a short welcome on the Home page, so that I know what ASA is and that I am welcome.\n62. As a visitor, I want the five latest Announcements on the Home page with a link to all of them, so that I can see what is new.\n63. As a visitor, I want an Announcements page listing all Published Posts, newest first by first-published date, so that I can browse.\n64. As a visitor, I want each Announcement as a clear Event card showing its title, first-published date and byline, so that I can scan quickly on a phone.\n65. As a visitor, I want an \"Updated\" date on a card when the content changed after first publishing, so that I know details were corrected.\n66. As a visitor, I want the event's actual date, time, location and who can attend in the Body, and the publication date never labelled as the event date, so that I am not misled.\n67. As a visitor, I want a Post to keep its original first-published date if it is unpublished and later published again, so that the order is stable.\n68. As a visitor, I want the Navigation to list Announcements and every published Page (About ASA, Resources, Photos for the demo), so that information is easy to find.\n69. As a visitor, I want the Photos page to say that actual event photos will be added when ASA has permission to use them, so that placeholders are not mistaken for real events.\n70. As a visitor, I want the public site to work on a phone with readable text, so that I can use it anywhere.\n71. As a visitor who uses a keyboard or screen reader, I want a sensible heading order, readable contrast, visible focus, keyboard access to all controls, and alt text on every image, so that the site is accessible.\n72. As a visitor, I want Drafts and unpublished content never to appear anywhere in the public site, so that unfinished work stays private.\n73. As a visitor, I want the site to work from a GitHub Pages subfolder, so that no link or style is broken.\n\n### Releasing\n74. As the release-holder, I want `cms publish` to write the public site from Published content only, so that Drafts never leave the database.\n75. As the release-holder, I want `cms deploy` to send the result to GitHub Pages, so that content becomes Live.\n76. As the release-holder, I want a short release checklist covering publish, deploy, checking relative paths, adding approved images, and confirming Photo permission, so that the step is quick and repeatable.\n77. As the release-holder, I want the checklist to state that an Editor cannot add an image file alone, so that clients are not surprised.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Architecture is fixed** by ADR-001 (local admin console, static public site) and ADR-002 (FastAPI, Jinja2, SQLite, argon2, Markdown sanitizer). No new dependencies.\n- **Content model.** Two kinds of content only: Post and Page. No separate Event type and no event-date field. A Post has a title, slug, Markdown Body, status (Draft or Published), Author, and created, updated and first-published times. A Page has the same fields except that the Author is optional for display and a Page does not appear in the Announcements list.\n- **First-published date** is set the first time a Post becomes Published and never cleared, including when the Post is unpublished and republished. The **Updated date** on the public card is shown when the Body or title changes after the first-published date.\n- **Ordering.** Announcements are ordered by first-published date, newest first. Editing does not reorder. The Home page shows the five most recent; the Announcements page shows all Published Posts.\n- **Slugs.** A Post's slug is generated from its title on first save, made unique by adding a number, and never changed automatically. A Page's slug is entered by the Admin. Only the Admin can change any slug, and the form warns that this can break existing links. Slug edits are not shown to Editors.\n- **Accounts.** An account has a private username, a password hash, a role (Admin or Editor), a public Display name, and an active flag. There is no account deletion. Posts keep their Author link permanently. A Post's public byline is the Author's current Display name. The username is never used as a byline.\n- **Authentication.** Session cookie signed by the server. Passwords are hashed with argon2 and are never stored, shown or logged in plain form. Sign-in failures use one generic message. A Deactivated account cannot sign in and any existing session for it stops working.\n- **Authorization.** A single central rule table maps each action to the roles allowed. Every state-changing route checks it on the server. Templates hide controls the current role cannot use, but hiding is never the protection. A refused action shows a \"You don't have permission\" page with a link to the dashboard, and a not-signed-in visitor is sent to sign-in.\n- **Permission matrix.** Admin: everything. Editor: sign in and out; create, edit and publish or unpublish Posts, including other people's; edit the Body of an existing Page; view dashboard counts and the Content list. Editor cannot: delete Posts or Pages; create, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug Pages; change any slug; open user management or change any account, role or Display name; reset passwords.\n- **CSRF.** Every state-changing form carries a token that the server verifies.\n- **Markdown and sanitizing.** Allowed: headings, emphasis, lists, links and images with alt text. Raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped. The same rendering and sanitizing path is used for the admin preview and the public export, so the preview matches the public page.\n- **Images.** No upload feature. Approved image files are added to the project by the release-holder and copied into the public site by `cms publish`. Markdown references use relative paths. Demo illustrations are clearly labelled illustrative.\n- **Admin console screens.** Sign-in, Dashboard, Content list, Post editor with Preview and the Event details hint, Page editor (reduced controls for Editors), User management (Admin only), and the permission-refusal page. Layouts work at phone width.\n- **Dashboard.** Four content counts for everyone; active and deactivated account counts for the Admin only; a reminder that Published is not Live. No \"Changes not live yet\" indicator.\n- **Content list.** One list of Posts and Pages, filterable by type and by status, with title, status, Display name and last-updated time on each row.\n- **Event details hint.** A short, dismissible hint near the Post editor listing What, When, Where and Who can attend. It is never inserted into the Body.\n- **Publishing.** `cms publish` renders Home, Announcements, each Published Post, each Published Page and the Navigation to plain HTML, using only Published content and only relative paths. It copies approved image files. `cms deploy` pushes the result to the `gh-pages` branch. Nothing is Live until both have run.\n- **Public design.** Polished, mobile-first, readable text, clear Event cards, a few appropriately licensed images or decorative graphics that are never presented as real ASA event photos. Accessibility requirements: sensible heading order, readable contrast, visible focus and full keyboard access, phone-width layout, and alt text on every image.\n- **Content carried from the client brief.** The Welcome text uses only the brief's wording. Starter Pages (About ASA, Resources, Photos) and seed Posts are demo placeholders and contain no claims about ASA's real history, officers, members, schedule or approval process.\n- **First Admin.** Created from the command line at setup, with the password read from the environment. No password, `.env` or database file is committed. A clean clone runs from `.env.example` plus documented steps.\n- **Demo data.** The seed data is rebuilt on demand and includes both roles, published and draft Posts and Pages, and a deactivated account.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test checks external behaviour only:** what a signed-in user sees and can do, and what files the publish step writes. It does not check internal functions, the database layout or template details.\n- **Seam 1: the admin console over HTTP.** Use the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. Cover sign-in and sign-out, Deactivated accounts, each row of the permission matrix (both the allowed and the refused case, for every Admin-only action), CSRF rejection, Post and Page create/read/update/delete, slug generation and stability, First-published date rules, Dashboard counts by role, Content list filtering, Preview matching public output, user management and Password reset, and Display name changes.\n- **Seam 2: the published site on disk.** Call the publish step against seeded data into a temporary folder and read the HTML files. Cover: Drafts and unpublished content absent; sanitized Markdown (raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms stripped); Navigation built from published Pages; Announcements ordering and the five-item Home page; byline uses the Display name and never the username; \"Updated\" date shown after later edits; only relative `href` and `src` values; approved image files copied; the Welcome text present.\n- **Seam 3 (small smoke test): the `cms` command.** First-Admin creation from an environment password, and a clean-setup run from `.env.example`. Kept small and separate.\n- **Prior art.** `tests/conftest.py` already provides the app and `client_as` fixtures, and the walking-skeleton test shows the style. Access-control tests should follow the one-line pattern in the `conftest.py` docstring.\n- **Test-first.** Each ticket starts with failing tests. Read the tests before letting them pass, to catch a test written against a misunderstanding.\n- **Security checks.** Drafts never appear in the export, no plain password in any response or log, no root-absolute paths in the export, sanitized output, CSRF on every state-changing form, and server-side enforcement of every Admin-only action.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- A separate Event content type, an event-date field, and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n- Photo upload forms, automatic gallery management, and photo approvals enforced by software. (The Photos page with approved images is in scope.)\n- Account deletion, and a first-login password change flow.","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"a8b5c7c3-2ad8-42a7-b82e-f589495c38cc","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:54:04.985Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"{\"body\":\"## Parent\\n\\nSpec: #1\\n\\n## What to build\\n\\nAn Editor can write a Post, save it as a Draft, edit it, and mark it Published. The permission rules exist in one central place, and an Editor who attempts an Admin-only action (deleting a Post, changing a slug) sees the \\\"You don't have permission\\\" page. As a visitor, I want the five latest Announcements on the Home page with a link to all of them, so that I can see what is new.\n63. As a visitor, I want an Announcements page listing all Published Posts, newest first by first-published date, so that I can browse.\n64. As a visitor, I want each Announcement as a clear Event card showing its title, first-published date and byline, so that I can scan quickly on a phone.\n65. As a visitor, I want an \"Updated\" date on a card when the content changed after first publishing, so that I know details were corrected.\n66. As a visitor, I want the event's actual date, time, location and who can attend in the Body, and the publication date never labelled as the event date, so that I am not misled.\n67. As a visitor, I want a Post to keep its original first-published date if it is unpublished and later published again, so that the order is stable.\n68. As a visitor, I want the Navigation to list Announcements and every published Page (About ASA, Resources, Photos for the demo), so that information is easy to find.\n69. As a visitor, I want the Photos page to say that actual event photos will be added when ASA has permission to use them, so that placeholders are not mistaken for real events.\n70. As a visitor, I want the public site to work on a phone with readable text, so that I can use it anywhere.\n71. As a visitor who uses a keyboard or screen reader, I want a sensible heading order, readable contrast, visible focus, keyboard access to all controls, and alt text on every image, so that the site is accessible.\n72. As a visitor, I want Drafts and unpublished content never to appear anywhere in the public site, so that unfinished work stays private.\n73. As a visitor, I want the site to work from a GitHub Pages subfolder, so that no link or style is broken.\n\n### Releasing\n74. As the release-holder, I want `cms publish` to write the public site from Published content only, so that Drafts never leave the database.\n75. As the release-holder, I want `cms deploy` to send the result to GitHub Pages, so that content becomes Live.\n76. As the release-holder, I want a short release checklist covering publish, deploy, checking relative paths, adding approved images, and confirming Photo permission, so that the step is quick and repeatable.\n77. As the release-holder, I want the checklist to state that an Editor cannot add an image file alone, so that clients are not surprised.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\n- **Architecture is fixed** by ADR-001 (local admin console, static public site) and ADR-002 (FastAPI, Jinja2, SQLite, argon2, Markdown sanitizer). No new dependencies.\n- **Content model.** Two kinds of content only: Post and Page. No separate Event type and no event-date field. A Post has a title, slug, Markdown Body, status (Draft or Published), Author, and created, updated and first-published times. A Page has the same fields except that the Author is optional for display and a Page does not appear in the Announcements list.\n- **First-published date** is set the first time a Post becomes Published and never cleared, including when the Post is unpublished and republished. The **Updated date** on the public card is shown when the Body or title changes after the first-published date.\n- **Ordering.** Announcements are ordered by first-published date, newest first. Editing does not reorder. The Home page shows the five most recent; the Announcements page shows all Published Posts.\n- **Slugs.** A Post's slug is generated from its title on first save, made unique by adding a number, and never changed automatically. A Page's slug is entered by the Admin. Only the Admin can change any slug, and the form warns that this can break existing links. Slug edits are not shown to Editors.\n- **Accounts.** An account has a private username, a password hash, a role (Admin or Editor), a public Display name, and an active flag. There is no account deletion. Posts keep their Author link permanently. A Post's public byline is the Author's current Display name. The username is never used as a byline.\n- **Authentication.** Session cookie signed by the server. Passwords are hashed with argon2 and are never stored, shown or logged in plain form. Sign-in failures use one generic message. A Deactivated account cannot sign in and any existing session for it stops working.\n- **Authorization.** A single central rule table maps each action to the roles allowed. Every state-changing route checks it on the server. Templates hide controls the current role cannot use, but hiding is never the protection. A refused action shows a \"You don't have permission\" page with a link to the dashboard, and a not-signed-in visitor is sent to sign-in.\n- **Permission matrix.** Admin: everything. Editor: sign in and out; create, edit and publish or unpublish Posts, including other people's; edit the Body of an existing Page; view dashboard counts and the Content list. Editor cannot: delete Posts or Pages; create, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug Pages; change any slug; open user management or change any account, role or Display name; reset passwords.\n- **CSRF.** Every state-changing form carries a token that the server verifies.\n- **Markdown and sanitizing.** Allowed: headings, emphasis, lists, links and images with alt text. Raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped. The same rendering and sanitizing path is used for the admin preview and the public export, so the preview matches the public page.\n- **Images.** No upload feature. Approved image files are added to the project by the release-holder and copied into the public site by `cms publish`. Markdown references use relative paths. Demo illustrations are clearly labelled illustrative.\n- **Admin console screens.** Sign-in, Dashboard, Content list, Post editor with Preview and the Event details hint, Page editor (reduced controls for Editors), User management (Admin only), and the permission-refusal page. Layouts work at phone width.\n- **Dashboard.** Four content counts for everyone; active and deactivated account counts for the Admin only; a reminder that Published is not Live. No \"Changes not live yet\" indicator.\n- **Content list.** One list of Posts and Pages, filterable by type and by status, with title, status, Display name and last-updated time on each row.\n- **Event details hint.** A short, dismissible hint near the Post editor listing What, When, Where and Who can attend. It is never inserted into the Body.\n- **Publishing.** `cms publish` renders Home, Announcements, each Published Post, each Published Page and the Navigation to plain HTML, using only Published content and only relative paths. It copies approved image files. `cms deploy` pushes the result to the `gh-pages` branch. Nothing is Live until both have run.\n- **Public design.** Polished, mobile-first, readable text, clear Event cards, a few appropriately licensed images or decorative graphics that are never presented as real ASA event photos. Accessibility requirements: sensible heading order, readable contrast, visible focus and full keyboard access, phone-width layout, and alt text on every image.\n- **Content carried from the client brief.** The Welcome text uses only the brief's wording. Starter Pages (About ASA, Resources, Photos) and seed Posts are demo placeholders and contain no claims about ASA's real history, officers, members, schedule or approval process.\n- **First Admin.** Created from the command line at setup, with the password read from the environment. No password, `.env` or database file is committed. A clean clone runs from `.env.example` plus documented steps.\n- **Demo data.** The seed data is rebuilt on demand and includes both roles, published and draft Posts and Pages, and a deactivated account.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\n- **A good test checks external behaviour only:** what a signed-in user sees and can do, and what files the publish step writes. It does not check internal functions, the database layout or template details.\n- **Seam 1: the admin console over HTTP.** Use the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures. Cover sign-in and sign-out, Deactivated accounts, each row of the permission matrix (both the allowed and the refused case, for every Admin-only action), CSRF rejection, Post and Page create/read/update/delete, slug generation and stability, First-published date rules, Dashboard counts by role, Content list filtering, Preview matching public output, user management and Password reset, and Display name changes.\n- **Seam 2: the published site on disk.** Call the publish step against seeded data into a temporary folder and read the HTML files. Cover: Drafts and unpublished content absent; sanitized Markdown (raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms stripped); Navigation built from published Pages; Announcements ordering and the five-item Home page; byline uses the Display name and never the username; \"Updated\" date shown after later edits; only relative `href` and `src` values; approved image files copied; the Welcome text present.\n- **Seam 3 (small smoke test): the `cms` command.** First-Admin creation from an environment password, and a clean-setup run from `.env.example`. Kept small and separate.\n- **Prior art.** `tests/conftest.py` already provides the app and `client_as` fixtures, and the walking-skeleton test shows the style. Access-control tests should follow the one-line pattern in the `conftest.py` docstring.\n- **Test-first.** Each ticket starts with failing tests. Read the tests before letting them pass, to catch a test written against a misunderstanding.\n- **Security checks.** Drafts never appear in the export, no plain password in any response or log, no root-absolute paths in the export, sanitized output, CSRF on every state-changing form, and server-side enforcement of every Admin-only action.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- A separate Event content type, an event-date field, and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n- Photo upload forms, automatic gallery management, and photo approvals enforced by software. (The Photos page with approved images is in scope.)\n- Account deletion, and a first-login password change flow.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"993dbd51-129d-4ab8-827f-ec9a6e3c1246","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

One short-lived connection per unit of work.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    username      TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    display_name  TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (active IN (0, 1)),\n    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    \"\"\"Open the database, creating the file and tables on first use.\n\n    Use as `with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:` so the connection is\n    closed and the transaction commits (or rolls back on error).\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    return conn\n\"\"\"Who is signed in, and CSRF protection.\n\nThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette SessionMiddleware). It holds only the\naccount id and a CSRF token. The account is looked up on every request, so a\nDeactivated account stops working immediately.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\nimport sqlite3\n\nfrom fastapi import HTTPException, Request\nfrom starlette.responses import RedirectResponse\nfrom starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send\n\nfrom app import accounts\n\nSAFE_METHODS = {\"GET\", \"HEAD\", \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active account for this request, or None.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = accounts.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on sign-in, so nothing from before it lives on.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef sign_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject any state-changing request that lacks this session's token.\n\n    Registered on the whole app, so a new route cannot forget it.\n    \"\"\"\n    if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:\n        return\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    form = await request.form()\n    supplied = form.get(\"csrf_token\") or \"\"\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(str(supplied), expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Invalid or missing CSRF token.\")\n\n\nclass AdminGuard:\n    \"\"\"Send anyone who is not signed in away from every /admin address.\n\n    A middleware rather than a per-route check, so it also covers addresses\n    that do not exist (they must not answer differently to a signed-out visitor).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:\n        self.app = app\n\n    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:\n        if scope[\"type\"] == \"http\" and (\n                scope[\"path\"] == \"/admin\" or scope[\"path\"].startswith(\"/admin/\")):\n            request = Request(scope, receive)\n            user = Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import security, settings\nfrom app.routes import admin, auth\nfrom app.web import templates\n\nSESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    # Every state-changing request is CSRF-checked, whatever route it reaches.\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\",\n                  dependencies=[Depends(security.verify_csrf)])\n    app.state.database = database or settings.DATABASE_PATH\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(admin.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Middleware added last is outermost: the session must be loaded before the\n    # guard runs, and saved after it.\n    app.add_middleware(security.AdminGuard)\n    app.add_middleware(\n        SessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY, session_cookie=\"cms_session\",\n        max_age=SESSION_LIFETIME_SECONDS, same_site=\"lax\",\n    )\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Accounts: creating them, hashing passwords, checking sign-in.\n\nA password is never stored, returned or logged in plain form; only the argon2\nhash is kept. PasswordHasher\nfrom argon2.exceptions import InvalidHashError, VerificationError\n\nfrom app.db import connect\n\nROLES = (\"admin\", \"editor\")\nMIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8\n\n_hasher = PasswordHasher()\n# Checked when the username is unknown, so a miss takes as long as a wrong\n# password and timing does not reveal which usernames exist.\n_DUMMY_HASH = pass\n\n\ndef hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n    return _hasher.hash(password)\n\n\ndef _password_matches(stored_hash: str, password: str) -> bool:\n    try:\n        return _hasher.verify(stored_hash, password)\n    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):\n        return False\n\n\ndef create_account(path: Path, *, username: str, password: str, role: str,\n Display name are required.\")\n    if role not in ROLES:\n        raise AccountError(f\"Role must be one of: {', '.join(ROLES)}.\")\n    if len(password) < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH:\n        raise AccountError(\n            f\"The password must be at least {MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.\")\n    try:\n        with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n            cursor = conn.execute(\n         )\n            return cursor.lastrowid\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise UsernameTaken(f\"An account named '{username}' already exists.\") from None\n\n\ndef authenticate(path: Path, username: str, password: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"Return the account for a correct username and password, else None.\n\n    An unknown username, a wrong password and a Deactivated account all return\n    None, so the caller can give one generic message for all three.\n    \"\"\"\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        user = conn.execute(\n closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef set_active(path: Path, username: str, active: bool) -> None:\n One short-lived connection per unit of work.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\n    id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,\n    username      TEXT    NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE,\n    password_hash TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    role          TEXT    NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'editor')),\n    display_name  TEXT    NOT NULL,\n    active        INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (active IN (0, 1)),\n    created_at    TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef connect(path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    \"\"\"Open the database, creating the file and tables on first use.\n\n    Use as `with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:` so the connection is\n    closed and the transaction commits (or rolls back on error).\n    \"\"\"\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.executescript(SCHEMA)\n    return conn\n\"\"\"Who is signed in, and CSRF protection.\n\nThe session is a signed cookie (Starlette SessionMiddleware). It holds only the\naccount id and a CSRF token. \"OPTIONS\"}\n\n\ndef current_user(request: Request) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    \"\"\"The signed-in, active account for this request, or None.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    if user_id is None:\n        return None\n    user = accounts.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id)\n    if user is None or not user[\"active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        return None\n    return user\n\n\ndef sign_in(request: Request, user: sqlite3.Row) -> None:\n    # A fresh session (and CSRF token) on sign-in, so nothing from before it lives on.\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user[\"id\"]\n\n\ndef sign_out(request: Request) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reject any state-changing request that lacks this session's token.\n\n    Registered on the whole app, so a new route cannot forget it.\n    \"\"\"\n    if request.method in sign in, sign out, Deactivated accounts, signed-out redirects, CSRF.\n\nSeam 1 (the admin console over HTTP): these tests only look at what a visitor\nsees and can do, plus the one stored-password check the security checklist asks\nfor (row 1).\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom app import accounts\nfrom tests.conftest import DEMO_USERS, csrf_token, token_in\n\n\ndef sign_in(client, username, password, token=None, **kw):\n    return client.post(\n        \"/login\",\n        data={\"username\": username, \"password\": password,\n              \"csrf_token\": token if token is not None else csrf_token(client)},\n        follow_redirects=False, **kw,\n    )\n\n\n# -- sign in ---------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_sign_in_page_has_a_form(client):\n    response = client.get(\"/login\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    assert 'name=\"username\"' in response.text\n    assert 'name=\"password\"' in response.text\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_correct_credentials_reach_the_dashboard(client, role):\n    user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/admin\"\n    dashboard = client.get(\"/admin\")\n    assert dashboard.status_code == 200\n    assert \"hello admin\" in dashboard.text.lower()\n\n\ndef test_username_is_not_case_sensitive(client):\n    response = sign_in(client, \"ADMIN\", DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n\n\ndef test_wrong_password_and_unknown_username_look_identical(client):\n    wrong_pw = sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    unknown = sign_in(client, \"nobody-here\", \"not-the-password\")\n    assert wrong_pw.status_code == unknown.status_code == 401\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in wrong_pw.text.lower()\n\n    def body(r):  # the CSRF token differs per response; nothing else may\n        return r.text.replace(token_in(r.text), \"\")\n\n    assert body(wrong_pw) == body(unknown)\n\n\ndef test_failed_sign_in_does_not_sign_in(client):\n    sign_in(client, \"admin\", \"not-the-password\")\n    response = client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n# -- Deactivated accounts --------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_deactivated_account_cannot_sign_in(client, db_path):\n    accounts.set_active(db_path, \"editor\", False)\n    user = DEMO_USERS[\"editor\"]\n    response = sign_in(client, user[\"username\"], user[\"password\"])\n    assert response.status_code == 401\n    # Same message as a wrong password: it must not reveal the account exists.\n    assert \"incorrect username or password\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert client.get(\"/admin\", follow_redirects=False).status_code in (302, 303)\n\n\n<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n  <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n  <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n  <title>{% block title %}{{ title }}{% endblock %}</title>\n  <link rel=\"stylesheet\" href=\"{{ css_path | default('style.css') }}\">\n</head>\n<body>\n  <header><a href=\"{{ home_path | default('index.html') }}\">{{ title }}</a></header>\n  <main>{% block content %}{% endblock %}</main>\n  <footer><small>IPHS 400 Mini-Project #2</small></footer>\n</body>\n</html>\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Dashboard</h1>\n  <p>Dashboard placeholder: hello admin console, {{ user.display_name }}.</p>\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/logout\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Sign out</button>\n  </form>\n{% endblock %}\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Sign in</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/login\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Username <input name=\"username\" autocomplete=\"username\" required autofocus></label></p>\n    <p><label>Password <input name=\"password\" type=\"password\" ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. {"parentUuid":"6483ae12-087e-48f2-b536-47a35035b551","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c6fedfd3-69a3-44f3-8484-8f7e881e0baf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_012eKJUpuqmxYJfJ5hNL7pLN","content":"Launching skill: mattpocock-skills:tdd"}]},"uuid":"1627fb77-47bc-40d3-baf3-b82385ca4061","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:54:14.096Z","toolUseResult":{"success":true,"commandName":"mattpocock-skills:tdd"},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6483ae12-087e-48f2-b536-47a35035b551","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"1627fb77-47bc-40d3-baf3-b82385ca4061","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c6fedfd3-69a3-44f3-8484-8f7e881e0baf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/yawoppong-krampah/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/tdd\n\n# Test-Driven Development\n\nTDD is the red → green loop. This skill is the reference that makes that loop produce tests worth keeping: what a good test is, where tests go, the anti-patterns, and the rules of the loop. Every section applies on every cycle: consult them before and during the loop, not after.\n\nWhen exploring the codebase, read `CONTEXT.md` (if it exists) so test names and interface vocabulary match the project's domain language, and respect ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n## What a good test is\n\nTests verify behavior through public interfaces, not implementation details. Code can change entirely; tests shouldn't. A good test reads like a specification: \"user can checkout with valid cart\" tells you exactly what capability exists, and it survives refactors because it doesn't care about internal structure.\n\nSee [tests.md](tests.md) for examples and [mocking.md](mocking.md) for mocking guidelines.\n\n## Seams: where tests go\n\nA **seam** is the public boundary you test at: the interface where you observe behavior without reaching inside. Tests live at seams, never against internals.\n\n**Test only at pre-agreed seams.** Before writing any test, write down the seams under test and confirm them with the user. No test is written at an unconfirmed seam. You can't test everything, so agreeing the seams up front is how testing effort lands on the critical paths and complex logic instead of every edge case.\n\nAsk: \"What's the public interface, and which seams should we test?\"\n\nWhen the shape of that interface is itself in question (how deep the module is, where the seam belongs, what the interface should expose), call the Skill tool with \"codebase-design\" for the vocabulary. It is the shared source of the module, interface, depth, seam, adapter, leverage and locality terms, and it is a reference to consult, not a session to run.\n\n## Anti-patterns\n\n- **Implementation-coupled**: mocks internal collaborators, tests private methods, or verifies through a side channel (querying the database instead of using the interface). The tell: the test breaks when you refactor but behavior hasn't changed.\n- **Tautological**: the assertion recomputes the expected value the way the code does (`expect(add(a, b)).toBe(a + b)`, a snapshot derived by hand the same way, a constant asserted equal to itself), so it passes by construction and can never disagree with the code. Expected values must come from an independent source of truth: a known-good literal, a worked example, the spec.\n- **Horizontal slicing**: writing all tests first, then all implementation. Bulk tests verify _imagined_ behavior: you test the _shape_ of things rather than user-facing behavior, the tests go insensitive to real changes, and you commit to test structure before understanding the implementation. Work in **vertical slices** instead: one test → one implementation → repeat, each test a **tracer bullet** that responds to what the last cycle taught you.\n\n## Rules of the loop\n\n- **Red before green.** Write the failing test first, then only enough code to pass it. Don't anticipate future tests or add speculative features.\n- **One slice at a time.** One seam, one test, one minimal implementation per cycle.\n- **Refactoring is not part of the loop.** It belongs to the review stage (see the `code-review` skill), not the red → green implementation cycle.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"91332665-029c-47de-9e41-d2abf79e7dc2","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:54:14.093Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_012eKJUpuqmxYJfJ5hNL7pLN","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b5b25de8-b659-4eec-a472-88fc96aab113","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWyYvm9oaUT7RxzxRrkm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012mt1baWcVawyb7jKf6aKoB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/tests/test_t02_posts.py","content":"\"\"\"T02: an Editor writes a Post, with roles and Permission refusal.\n\nSeam 1 (the admin console over HTTP): everything here is what a signed-in\nperson sees and can do, or what the server answers to a request sent directly.\n\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport time\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_token, token_in\n\nREFUSAL = \"you don't have permission\"\n\n\ndef create_post(client, title=\"Afrobeat Night\", body=\"What: music\", **kw):\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/admin/posts\",\n        data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body,\n              \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(client, \"/admin/posts/new\")},\n        follow_redirects=False, **kw,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_to(client, path, **data):\n    data[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token(client, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return client.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_page(client, post_id):\n    response = client.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.text\n\n\ndef time_of(html, name):\n    \"\"\"The machine-readable time in <time id=\"name\" datetime=\"...\">, or None.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(rf'<time id=\"{name}\"[^>]*datetime=\"([^\"]*)\"', html)\n    return match.group(1) if match else None\n\n\ndef slug_of(html):\n    match = re.search(r'id=\"post-slug\"[^>]*>([^<]+)<', html)\n    assert match, \"the slug is not shown\"\n    return match.group(1).strip()\n\n\ndef is_refusal(response):\n    return response.status_code == 403 and REFUSAL in response.text.lower()\n\n\n# -- writing, drafts, editing assert post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\").status_code == 303\n    assert post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\").status_code == 303\n\n\ndef assert is_refusal(response)\n    assert 'href=\"/admin\"' in response.text\n\n\n# -- signed-out visitors \"/admin/posts/1/edit\"),\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/posts/1/slug\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/publish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/delete\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/slug\"),\n])\ndef test_signed_out_visitors_are_sent_to_sign_in(client, method, path):\n    response = getattr(client, method)(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n# -- CSRF ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_post_form_without_a_token_is_rejected_and_changes_nothing(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = create_post(admin, \"Keep me\")\n    for path, data in [\n        (\"/admin/posts\", {\"title\": \"Sneaky\", \"body\": \"x\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", {\"title\": \"Changed\", \"body\": \"x\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\", {}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\", {}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/slug\", {\"slug\": \"changed\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/delete\", {}),\n    ]:\n        response = admin.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code == 403, path\n    assert \"Keep me\" in edit_page(admin, post_id)\n    assert \"Sneaky\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert slug_of(edit_page(admin, post_id)) == \"keep-me\"\n\n\ndef test_a_wrong_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = admin.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body\": \"y\", \"csrf_token\": \"nope\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_every_post_form_carries_a_csrf_token(client_as, role):\n    client = client_as(role)\n    post_id = create_post(client)\n    pages = [\"/admin/posts/new\", f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\", \"/admin/posts\"]\n    if role == \"admin\":\n        pages.append(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/slug\")\n    for path in pages:\n        html = client.get(path).text\n        forms = re.findall(r\"<form\\b[^>]*method=\\\"post\\\"[^>]*>.*?</form>\", html, an Editor writes a Post, with roles and Permission refusal.\n\nSeam 1 (the admin console over HTTP): everything here is what a signed-in\nperson sees and can do, or what the server answers to a request sent directly.\n\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport time\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom tests.conftest import csrf_token, token_in\n\nREFUSAL = \"you don't have permission\"\n\n\ndef create_post(client, title=\"Afrobeat Night\", body=\"What: music\", **kw):\n    response = client.post(\n        \"/admin/posts\",\n        data={\"title\": title, \"body\": body,\n              \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(client, \"/admin/posts/new\")},\n        follow_redirects=False, **kw,\n    )\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/posts/(\\d+)/edit\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef post_to(client, path, **data):\n    data[\"csrf_token\"] = csrf_token(client, \"/admin/posts/new\")\n    return client.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef edit_page(client, post_id):\n    response = client.get(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\")\n    assert response.status_code == 200\n    return response.text\n\n\ndef time_of(html, name):\n    \"\"\"The machine-readable time in <time id=\"name\" datetime=\"...\">, or None.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(rf'<time id=\"{name}\"[^>]*datetime=\"([^\"]*)\"', html)\n    return match.group(1) if match else None\n\n\ndef slug_of(html):\n    match = re.search(r'id=\"post-slug\"[^>]*>([^<]+)<', html)\n    assert match, \"the slug is not shown\"\n    return match.group(1).strip()\n\n\ndef is_refusal(response):\n    return response.status_code == 403 and REFUSAL in response.text.lower()\n\n\n# -- writing, drafts, editing ----------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_editor_creates_a_draft_and_it_is_listed(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor, \"Afrobeat Night\", \"What: music\\n\\nWhere: Gund\")\n    html = edit_page(editor, post_id)\n    assert \"Afrobeat Night\" in html\n    assert \"What: music\" in html\n    assert \"draft\" in html.lower()\n    assert \"Afrobeat Night\" in editor.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n\n\ndef test_author_is_the_signed_in_account_and_needs_no_form_field(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert 'name=\"author' not in editor.get(\"/admin/posts/new\").text\n    html = edit_page(editor, create_post(editor))\n    assert \"ASA Events Team\" in html      # the editor's Display name\n    assert \"ASA Leadership\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_posts_record_created_and_updated_times_automatically(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    html = edit_page(editor, create_post(editor))\n    assert time_of(html, \"created\")\n    assert time_of(html, \"updated\")\n\n\ndef test_editing_saves_the_new_title_and_body(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor, \"Old title\", \"old body\")\n    before = time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"updated\")\n    time.sleep(0.01)\n    response = post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", title=\"New title\", body=\"new body\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    html = edit_page(editor, post_id)\n    assert \"New title\" in html and \"new body\" in html\n    assert \"Old title\" not in html\n    assert time_of(html, \"updated\") > before\n\n\ndef test_a_post_needs_a_title(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_to(editor, \"/admin/posts\", title=\"   \", body=\"text\")\n    assert response.status_code == 400\n    assert \"title\" in response.text.lower()\n    assert \"text\" in response.text            # what was typed is not lost\n\n\ndef test_editor_can_edit_a_post_written_by_someone_else(client_as):\n    post_id = create_post(client_as(\"admin\"), \"Admin's post\", \"original\")\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    response = post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", title=\"Corrected\", body=\"fixed time\")\n    assert response.status_code == 303\n    assert \"fixed time\" in edit_page(editor, post_id)\n    # ...and the Author is still the person who wrote it.\n    assert \"ASA Leadership\" in edit_page(editor, post_id)\n\n\ndef test_editing_a_missing_post_is_not_found(client_as):\n    assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/posts/9999/edit\").status_code == 404\n\n\n# -- Draft / Published -----------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_publish_and_unpublish_change_the_status(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor)\n    assert \"not yet published\" in edit_page(editor, post_id).lower()\n\n    assert post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\").status_code == 303\n    published = edit_page(editor, post_id)\n    assert 'id=\"post-status\"' in published\n    assert re.search(r'id=\"post-status\"[^>]*>\\s*Published', published)\n\n    assert post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\").status_code == 303\n    assert re.search(r'id=\"post-status\"[^>]*>\\s*Draft', edit_page(editor, post_id))\n\n\ndef test_first_published_date_is_set_once_and_kept(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor)\n    assert time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\") is None\n\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\")\n    first = time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\")\n    assert first\n\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\")\n    assert time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\") == first\n\n    time.sleep(0.01)\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\")\n    assert time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\") == first\n\n\ndef test_publishing_twice_does_not_move_the_first_published_date(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor)\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\")\n    first = time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\")\n    time.sleep(0.01)\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\")\n    assert time_of(edit_page(editor, post_id), \"first-published\") == first\n\n\n# -- slugs -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_slug_is_generated_from_the_title(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor, \"Afrobeat Night!  (Friday)\")\n    assert slug_of(edit_page(editor, post_id)) == \"afrobeat-night-friday\"\n\n\ndef test_a_number_is_added_when_the_slug_exists(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    slugs = [slug_of(edit_page(editor, create_post(editor, \"Afrobeat Night\")))\n             for _ in range(3)]\n    assert slugs == [\"afrobeat-night\", \"afrobeat-night-2\", \"afrobeat-night-3\"]\n\n\ndef test_a_title_with_no_letters_still_gets_a_slug(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert slug_of(edit_page(editor, create_post(editor, \"!!!\")))\n\n\ndef test_slug_does_not_change_when_the_title_is_edited(client_as):\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = create_post(editor, \"Afrobeat Night\")\n    post_to(editor, f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", title=\"Afrobeat Night (moved to Saturday)\", body=\"x\")\n    assert slug_of(edit_page(editor, post_id)) == \"afrobeat-night\"\n\n\ndef ---------------------------------------------------\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"method,path\", [\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/posts\"),\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/posts/new\"),\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/posts/1/edit\"),\n    (\"get\", \"/admin/posts/1/slug\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/publish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/unpublish\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/delete\"),\n    (\"post\", \"/admin/posts/1/slug\"),\n])\ndef test_signed_out_visitors_are_sent_to_sign_in(client, method, path):\n    response = getattr(client, method)(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert response.status_code in (302, 303)\n    assert response.headers[\"location\"] == \"/login\"\n\n\n# -- CSRF ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndef test_a_post_form_without_a_token_is_rejected_and_changes_nothing(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    post_id = create_post(admin, \"Keep me\")\n    for path, data in [\n        (\"/admin/posts\", {\"title\": \"Sneaky\", \"body\": \"x\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", {\"title\": \"Changed\", \"body\": \"x\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/publish\", {}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/unpublish\", {}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/slug\", {\"slug\": \"changed\"}),\n        (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/delete\", {}),\n    ]:\n        response = admin.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n        assert response.status_code == 403, path\n    assert \"Keep me\" in edit_page(admin, post_id)\n    assert \"Sneaky\" not in admin.get(\"/admin/posts\").text\n    assert slug_of(edit_page(admin, post_id)) == \"keep-me\"\n\n\ndef test_a_wrong_token_is_rejected(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    response = admin.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"x\", \"body\": \"y\", \"csrf_token\": \"nope\"})\n    assert response.status_code == 403\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"role\", [\"admin\", \"editor\"])\ndef test_every_post_form_carries_a_csrf_token(client_as, role):\n    client = client_as(role)\n    post_id = create_post(client)\n    pages = [\"/admin/posts/new\", f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\", \"/admin/posts\"]\n    if role == \"admin\":\n        pages.append(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/slug\")\n    for path in pages:\n        html = client.get(path).text\n        forms = re.findall(r\"<form\\b[^>]*method=\\\"post\\\"[^>]*>.*?</form>\", html, re.S)\n        for form in forms:\n            assert token_in(form), f\"a form on {path} has no CSRF Templates ask\n`can(role, action)` only to hide controls; hiding is never the protection.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import Request\n\nADMIN = \"admin\"\nEDITOR = \"editor\"\nEVERYONE = frozenset({ADMIN, EDITOR})\nADMIN_ONLY = frozenset({ADMIN})\n\nRULES: dict[str, frozenset[str]] = {\n    \"post.list\": EVERYONE,\n    \"post.create\": EVERYONE,\n    \"post.edit\": EVERYONE,\n    \"post.publish\": EVERYONE,\n    \"post.unpublish\": EVERYONE,\n    \"post.delete\": ADMIN_ONLY,\n    \"post.change_slug\": ADMIN_ONLY,\n}\n\n\nclass PermissionRefused(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The signed-in account's role may not do this action.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef allowed(role: str, action: str) -> bool:\n    # An action missing from the table is refused, never allowed by accident.\n    return role in RULES.get(action, frozenset())\n\n\ndef require(action: str):\n    \"\"\"A route dependency: refuse the request unless the role may `action`.\n\n    Runs before the route looks anything up, so an Editor gets the same\n    refusal whether or not the thing they asked about exists.\n    \"\"\"\n    if action not in RULES:\n        raise KeyError(f\"unknown action: {action}\")\n\n    def check(request: Request) -> None:\n        if not allowed(request.state.user[\"role\"], action):\n            raise PermissionRefused(action)\n\n    return check\nEOF\ncat > app/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Posts: the data rules (slugs, Author, First-published date).\n\nNothing here knows about HTTP. Slugs are made once from the title and only\nchange through `change_slug`, which the routes reserve for the Admin.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom contextlib import closing\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.db import connect\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A request about a Post that cannot be honoured (shown to the person).\"\"\"\n\n\nclass TitleRequired(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass SlugInvalid(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass SlugTaken(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"microseconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    ascii_text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", ascii_text.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, base: str) -> str:\n    slug, number = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        number += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{number}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise TitleRequired(\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    return title\n\n\n_WITH_AUTHOR = (\"SELECT posts.*, users.display_name AS author_name \"\n                \"FROM posts JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id\")\n\n\ndef create(path: Path, *, title: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    now = _now()\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        slug = _unique_slug(conn, slugify(title) or \"post\")\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at, updated_at) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, slug, body, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n        return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get(path: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_WITH_AUTHOR} WHERE posts.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all(path: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_WITH_AUTHOR} ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\"\n                            ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef update(path: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str) -> None:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (title, body, _now(), post_id))\n\n\ndef publish(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark Published. The First-published date is set once and never moved.\"\"\"\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', \"\n            \"first_published_at = COALESCE(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (_now(), post_id))\n\n\ndef unpublish(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft' WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef change_slug(path: Path, post_id: int, slug: str) -> None:\n    slug = slugify(slug)\n    if not slug:\n        raise SlugInvalid(\"A slug needs at least one letter or number.\")\n    try:\n        with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n            conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET slug = ? WHERE id = ?\", (slug, post_id))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise SlugTaken(f\"Another Post already uses '{slug}'.\") from None\nEOF","description":"Add posts schema, permission table, and data layer"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":914,"cache_read_input_tokens":76679,"output_tokens":4769,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":2141},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":914,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":4769,"cache_read_input_tokens":76679,"cache_creation_input_tokens":914,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":914},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_019QvF5sv8dqzpYUvpk7TxHb":{"command":"cd /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && python3 -
