{"parentUuid":"9dc96842-fa14-4164-aef3-ea4e7dea7958","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWyfMB1VJbFYPux2AKep","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Qn9j7aDzbQXjftLSzGL3nm","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > app/routes/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Post screens: list, write, edit, publish, delete, and the Admin-only slug.\n\nEach route names its action in `Depends(require(...))`; the rule table in\napp/permissions.py decides who may. Permission is checked before the Post is\nlooked up.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import posts\nfrom app.permissions import require\nfrom app.web import render\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\n\n\ndef _db(request: Request):\n    return request.app.state.database\n\n\ndef _edit_url(post_id: int) -> str:\n    return f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}/edit\"\n\n\ndef _found(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    post = posts.get(_db(request), post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"No such Post.\")\n    return post\n\n\ndef _form(request: Request, *, post=None, status_code=200, **context):\n    return render(request, \"admin/post_form.html\", status_code=status_code,\n                  title=\"Edit Post\" if post else \"New Post\", user=request.state.user,\n                  post=post, **context)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.list\"))])\ndef post_list(request: Request):\n    return render(request, \"admin/post_list.html\", title=\"Posts\",\n                  user=request.state.user, posts=posts.list_all(_db(request)))\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.create\"))])\ndef new_post(request: Request):\n    return _form(request, title_value=\"\", body_value=\"\")\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.create\"))])\ndef create_post(request: Request, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    try:\n        post_id = posts.create(_db(request), title=title, body=body,\n                               author_id=request.state.user[\"id\"])\n    except posts.PostError as error:\n        return _form(request, status_code=400, error=str(error),\n                     title_value=title, body_value=body)\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/edit\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.edit\"))])\ndef edit_post(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    post = _found(request, post_id)\n    return _form(request, post=post, title_value=post[\"title\"], body_value=post[\"body\"])\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.edit\"))])\ndef save_post(request: Request, post_id: int, title: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\")):\n    post = _found(request, post_id)\n    try:\n        posts.update(_db(request), post_id, title=title, body=body)\n    except posts.PostError as error:\n        return _form(request, post=post, status_code=400, error=str(error),\n                     title_value=title, body_value=body)\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/publish\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.publish\"))])\ndef publish_post(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    _found(request, post_id)\n    posts.publish(_db(request), post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/unpublish\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.unpublish\"))])\ndef unpublish_post(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    _found(request, post_id)\n    posts.unpublish(_db(request), post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id), status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/delete\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.delete\"))])\ndef delete_post(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    _found(request, post_id)\n    posts.delete(_db(request), post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id}/slug\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.change_slug\"))])\ndef slug_form(request: Request, post_id: int):\n    # The slug form lives on the edit page, beside its warning.\n    _found(request, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id) + \"#slug\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id}/slug\", dependencies=[Depends(require(\"post.change_slug\"))])\ndef change_slug(request: Request, post_id: int, slug: str = Form(\"\")):\n    post = _found(request, post_id)\n    try:\n        posts.change_slug(_db(request), post_id, slug)\n    except posts.PostError as error:\n        code = 409 if isinstance(error, posts.SlugTaken) else 400\n        return _form(request, post=post, status_code=code, slug_error=str(error),\n                     title_value=post[\"title\"], body_value=post[\"body\"])\n    return RedirectResponse(_edit_url(post_id), status_code=303)\nEOF\npython3 - <<'EOF'\np='app/main.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"from app.routes import admin, \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts\">All Posts</a></p>\n  <h1>{% if post %}Edit Post{% else %}New Post{% endif %}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{% if post %}/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}{% else %}/admin/posts{% endif %}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Title <input name=\"title\" value=\"{{ title_value }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n      <textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"14\" cols=\"60\">{{ body_value }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">{% if post %}Save changes{% else %}Save as Draft{% endif %}</button></p>\n  </form>\n\n  {% if post %}\n  <dl>\n    <dt>Status</dt><dd><strong id=\"post-status\">{{ post.status | capitalize }}</strong></dd>\n    <dt>Author</dt><dd>{{ post.author_name }}</dd>\n    <dt>Slug</dt><dd><code id=\"post-slug\">{{ post.slug }}</code></dd>\n    <dt>Created</dt><dd><time id=\"created\" datetime=\"{{ post.created_at }}\">{{ post.created_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time></dd>\n    <dt>Updated</dt><dd><time id=\"updated\" datetime=\"{{ post.updated_at }}\">{{ post.updated_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time></dd>\n    <dt>First published</dt>\n    <dd>{% if post.first_published_at %}<time id=\"first-published\" datetime=\"{{ post.first_published_at }}\">{{ post.first_published_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time>{% else %}Not yet published{% endif %}</dd>\n  </dl>\n\n  {% if post.status == 'draft' %}\n  {% if can(user.role, 'post.publish') %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/publish\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Mark Published</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% elif can(user.role, 'post.unpublish') %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id posts %}\n  <ul>\n    {% for post in posts %}\n    <li>\n      <a href=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/edit\">{{ post.title }}</a>\n      &middot; {{ post.status | capitalize }} &middot; {{ \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <p><a href=\"/admin/posts\">All Posts</a></p>\n  <h1>{% if post %}Edit Post{% else %}New Post{% endif %}</h1>\n  {% if error %}<p role=\"alert\">{{ error }}</p>{% endif %}\n\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"{% if post %}/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}{% else %}/admin/posts{% endif %}\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <p><label>Title <input name=\"title\" value=\"{{ title_value }}\" required></label></p>\n    <p><label>Body (Markdown)<br>\n      <textarea name=\"body\" rows=\"14\" cols=\"60\">{{ body_value }}</textarea></label></p>\n    <p><button type=\"submit\">{% if post %}Save changes{% else %}Save as Draft{% endif %}</button></p>\n  </form>\n\n  {% if post %}\n  <dl>\n    <dt>Status</dt><dd><strong id=\"post-status\">{{ post.status | capitalize }}</strong></dd>\n    <dt>Author</dt><dd>{{ post.author_name }}</dd>\n    <dt>Slug</dt><dd><code id=\"post-slug\">{{ post.slug }}</code></dd>\n    <dt>Created</dt><dd><time id=\"created\" datetime=\"{{ post.created_at }}\">{{ post.created_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time></dd>\n    <dt>Updated</dt><dd><time id=\"updated\" datetime=\"{{ post.updated_at }}\">{{ post.updated_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time></dd>\n    <dt>First published</dt>\n    <dd>{% if post.first_published_at %}<time id=\"first-published\" datetime=\"{{ post.first_published_at }}\">{{ post.first_published_at[:16] | replace('T', ' ') }} UTC</time>{% else %}Not yet published{% endif %}</dd>\n  </dl>\n\n  {% if csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Mark Published</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n  {% elif can(user.role, 'post.unpublish') %}\n  <form method=\"post\" action=\"/admin/posts/{{ post.id }}/unpublish\">\n    <input type=\"hidden\" name=\"csrf_token\" value=\"{{ csrf_token }}\">\n    <button type=\"submit\">Unpublish</button>\n  </form>\n  {% endif %}\n\n  {% if can(user.role, {"parentUuid":"4741f852-2f77-43f4-9fdf-4d358fed44c6","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWymtEhvMsTHiDuNiqiF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018mtm23B43ZVDqWqxn5WyzS","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"head -5 app/posts.py && cat > app/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Posts: the data rules (slugs, Author, First-published date).\n\nNothing here knows about HTTP. Slugs are made once from the title and only\nchange through `change_slug`, which the routes reserve for the Admin.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nimport unicodedata\nfrom contextlib import closing\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.db import connect\n\n\nclass PostError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A request about a Post that cannot be honoured (shown to the person).\"\"\"\n\n\nclass TitleRequired(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass SlugInvalid(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass SlugTaken(PostError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"microseconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(text: str) -> str:\n    ascii_text = unicodedata.normalize(\"NFKD\", text).encode(\"ascii\", \"ignore\").decode()\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", ascii_text.lower()).strip(\"-\")\n\n\ndef _unique_slug(conn: sqlite3.Connection, base: str) -> str:\n    slug, number = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"SELECT 1 FROM posts WHERE slug = ?\", (slug,)).fetchone():\n        number += 1\n        slug = f\"{base}-{number}\"\n    return slug\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise TitleRequired(\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    return title\n\n\n_WITH_AUTHOR = (\"SELECT posts.*, users.display_name AS author_name \"\n                \"FROM posts JOIN users ON users.id = posts.author_id\")\n\n\ndef create(path: Path, *, title: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    now = _now()\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        slug = _unique_slug(conn, slugify(title) or \"post\")\n        cursor = conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at, updated_at) \"\n            \"VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, slug, body, author_id, now, now),\n        )\n        return cursor.lastrowid\n\n\ndef get(path: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_WITH_AUTHOR} WHERE posts.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_all(path: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_WITH_AUTHOR} ORDER BY posts.updated_at DESC, posts.id DESC\"\n                            ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef update(path: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str) -> None:\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET title = ?, body = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?\",\n                     (title, body, _now(), post_id))\n\n\ndef publish(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Mark Published. The First-published date is set once and never moved.\"\"\"\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\n            \"UPDATE posts SET status = 'published', \"\n            \"first_published_at = COALESCE(first_published_at, ?) WHERE id = ?\",\n            (_now(), post_id))\n\n\ndef unpublish(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft' WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef change_slug(path: Path, post_id: int, slug: str) -> None:\n    slug = slugify(slug)\n    if not slug:\n        raise SlugInvalid(\"A slug needs at least one letter or number.\")\n    try:\n        with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n            conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET slug = ? WHERE id = ?\", (slug, post_id))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise SlugTaken(f\"Another Post already uses '{slug}'.\") from None\nEOF\ngit status --short; grep -n \"ADMIN_ONLY\\|EVERYONE\" app/permissions.py | head; grep -c \"can(user.role, 'post.delete')\" templates/admin/post_form.html\nuv run pytest tests/test_t02_posts.py -q 2>&1 | grep -E \"passed|failed\"","description":"Restore clobbered posts.py and re-run tests"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1324,"cache_read_input_tokens":86859,"output_tokens":2164,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":352},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1324,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2164,"cache_read_input_tokens":86859,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1324,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1324},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_018mtm23B43ZVDqWqxn5WyzS":{"command":"cd /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && head -5 app/posts.py && cat > app/posts.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"Posts: the data rules (slugs, Author, First-published date).\n\nNothing here knows about HTTP. WHERE id = ?\",\n            (_now(), post_id))\n\n\ndef unpublish(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET status = 'draft' WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete(path: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n        conn.execute(\"DELETE FROM posts WHERE id = ?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef change_slug(path: Path, post_id: int, slug: str) -> None:\n    slug = slugify(slug)\n    if not slug:\n        raise SlugInvalid(\"A slug needs at least one letter or number.\")\n    try:\n        with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:\n            conn.execute(\"UPDATE posts SET slug = ? WHERE id = ?\", (slug, post_id))\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:\n        raise SlugTaken(f\"Another Post already uses '{slug}'.\") from None\nEOF\ngit status --short; grep -n \"ADMIN_ONLY\\|EVERYONE\" app/permissions.py | head; grep -c \"can(user.role, 'post.delete')\" templates/admin/post_form.html\nuv run pytest tests/test_t02_posts.py -q 2>&1 | grep -E \"passed|failed\"","description":"Restore clobbered posts.py and re-run tests"}},"wireIngestContext":{"toolu_018mtm23B43ZVDqWqxn5WyzS":{"cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms"}},"apiBlockIndex":2,"requestId":"req_011CfWymsBUAb4iAuXMR8YYk","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:tdd","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"fa8ba3f5-8fe6-4c56-97ef-cce49592ee93","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:57:22.330Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"4b99fa75-cc2f-4090-ac7e-cfe2e2dc5054","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"c6fedfd3-69a3-44f3-8484-8f7e881e0baf","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/yawoppong-krampah/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/code-review\n\nTwo-axis review of the diff between `HEAD` and a fixed point the user supplies:\n\n- **Standards**: does the code conform to this repo's documented coding standards?\n- **Spec**: does the code faithfully implement the originating issue / spec?\n\nBoth axes run as **parallel sub-agents** so they don't pollute each other's context, then this skill aggregates their findings.\n\nThe issue tracker should have been provided to you. If `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md` is missing, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Pin the fixed point\n\nWhatever the user said is the fixed point (a commit SHA, branch name, tag, `main`, `HEAD~5`, etc.). If they didn't specify one, ask for it.\n\nCapture the diff command once: `git diff <fixed-point>...HEAD` (three-dot, so the comparison is against the merge-base). Also note the list of commits via `git log <fixed-point>..HEAD --oneline`.\n\nBefore going further, confirm the fixed point resolves (`git rev-parse <fixed-point>`) and the diff is non-empty. A bad ref or empty diff should fail here, not inside two parallel sub-agents.\n\n### 2. Identify the spec source\n\nLook for the originating spec, in this order:\n\n1. Issue references in the commit messages (`#123`, `Closes #45`, GitLab `!67`, etc.), fetched via the workflow in `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\n2. A path the user passed as an argument.\n3. A spec file under `docs/`, `specs/`, or `.scratch/` matching the branch name or feature.\n4. If nothing is found, ask the user where the spec is. If they say there isn't one, the **Spec** sub-agent will skip and report \"no spec available\".\n\n### 3. Identify the standards sources\n\nAnything in the repo that documents how code should be written, such as `CODING_STANDARDS.md` or `CONTRIBUTING.md`.\n\nOn top of whatever the repo documents, the Standards axis always carries the **smell baseline** below: a fixed set of Fowler code smells (_Refactoring_, ch.3) that applies even when a repo documents nothing. Two rules bind it:\n\n- **The repo overrides.** A documented repo standard always wins; where it endorses something the baseline would flag, suppress the smell.\n- **Always a judgement call.** Each smell is a labelled heuristic (\"possible Feature Envy\"), never a hard violation. Like any standard here, skip anything tooling already enforces.\n\nEach smell reads *what it is* → *how to fix*; match it against the diff:\n\n- **Mysterious Name**: a function, variable, or type whose name doesn't reveal what it does or holds. → rename it; if no honest name comes, the design's murky.\n- **Duplicated Code**: the same logic shape appears in more than one hunk or file in the change. → extract the shared shape, call it from both.\n- **Feature Envy**: a method that reaches into another object's data more than its own. → move the method onto the data it envies.\n- **Data Clumps**: the same few fields or params keep travelling together (a type wanting to be born). → bundle them into one type, pass that.\n- **Primitive Obsession**: a primitive or string standing in for a domain concept that deserves its own type. → give the concept its own small type.\n- **Repeated Switches**: the same `switch`/`if`-cascade on the same type recurs across the change. → replace with polymorphism, or one map both sites share.\n- **Shotgun Surgery**: one logical change forces scattered edits across many files in the diff. → gather what changes together into one module.\n- **Divergent Change**: one file or module is edited for several unrelated reasons. → split so each module changes for one reason.\n- **Speculative Generality**: abstraction, parameters, or hooks added for needs the spec doesn't have. → delete it; inline back until a real need shows.\n- **Message Chains**: long `a.b().c().d()` navigation the caller shouldn't depend on. → hide the walk behind one method on the first object.\n- **Middle Man**: a class or function that mostly just delegates onward. → cut it, call the real target direct.\n- **Refused Bequest**: a subclass or implementer that ignores or overrides most of what it inherits. → drop the inheritance, use composition.\n\n### 4. Spawn both sub-agents in parallel\n\n**Standards sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The full diff command and commit list.\n- The list of standards-source files you found in step 3, **plus the smell baseline from step 3** pasted in full (the sub-agent has no other access to it).\n- The brief: \"Report, per file/hunk where relevant, (a) every place the diff violates a documented standard: cite the standard (file + the rule); and (b) any baseline smell you spot: name it and quote the hunk. Distinguish hard violations from judgement calls: documented-standard breaches can be hard, but baseline smells are always judgement calls, and a documented repo standard overrides the baseline. Skip anything tooling enforces. Under 400 words.\"\n\n**Spec sub-agent prompt** should include:\n\n- The diff command and commit list.\n- The path or fetched contents of the spec.\n- The brief: \"Report: (a) requirements the spec asked for that are missing or partial; (b) behaviour in the diff that wasn't asked for (scope creep); (c) requirements that look implemented but where the implementation looks wrong. Quote the spec line for each finding. Under 400 words.\"\n\nIf the spec is missing, skip the Spec sub-agent and note this in the final report.\n\n### 5. Aggregate\n\nPresent the two reports under `## Standards` and `## Spec` headings, verbatim or lightly cleaned. Do **not** merge or rerank findings, because the two axes are deliberately separate (see _Why two axes_).\n\nEnd with a one-line summary: total findings per axis, and the worst issue _within each axis_ (if any). Don't pick a single winner across axes: that's the reranking the separation exists to prevent.\n\n## Why two axes\n\nA change can pass one axis and fail the other:\n\n- Code that follows every standard but implements the wrong thing → **Standards pass, Spec fail.**\n- Code that does exactly what the issue asked but breaks the project's conventions → **Spec pass, Standards fail.**\n\nReporting them separately stops one axis from masking the other.\n\n\nARGUMENTS: since HEAD (uncommitted T02 work, ticket #3, spec #1)"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"2b24ac20-aa6a-4f61-9660-0190cca0e473","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:58:15.799Z","sourceToolUseID":"toolu_013ShWnAQL2pN1cxMfAgnNze","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Standards review of the T02 diff (read-only)**\n\n**(a) Violations of documented standards**\n\nHard violations: none found.\n- CLAUDE.md \"Every state-changing form carries a CSRF token\": all 6 POST forms carry it, and `test_every_post_form_carries_a_csrf_token` covers them.\n- CLAUDE.md \"Ask before adding a dependency\": none added.\n- CLAUDE.md \"Never edit `site/` by hand\" and \"Only published content reaches `site/`\": neither is touched.\n- CLAUDE.md \"Published HTML uses relative paths only\": the absolute `/admin/...` hrefs are admin-console templates, not published output. They match existing code such as `/logout`.\n- SECURITY-CHECKLIST #4, #5, #10: access control is enforced server-side through `require()`, and tests hit the URLs directly.\n\nJudgement calls against CONTEXT.md:\n- `post_list.html` does not show the last-updated time. CONTEXT \"Content list\" says each row shows title, status, Display name and last-updated time. It may be out of scope for T02, so check it against the ticket.\n- The Author is called `author_name` in the SQL alias and templates, and the UI label is \"Author\". CONTEXT defines Display name as the public byline. Consider `display_name` or a comment.\n- `post_form.html` shows \"Draft\" and \"Published\" via `status | capitalize`. That matches the glossary. Nothing in the diff says \"Live\" when it means Published.\n- Slug is Admin-only and the warning is shown, both as CONTEXT requires.\n- CLAUDE.md says a commit that closes a ticket needs the `/code-review` comment first. That is process, not code.\n\n**(b) Baseline smells (all judgement calls)**\n\n- **Duplicated Code**\n  - `_found(request, post_id)` is followed by a one-line `posts.X(...)` call in `publish_post`, `unpublish_post` and `delete_post`.\n  - In `app/posts.py`, `with closing(connect(path)) as conn, conn:` appears in 6 functions.\n  - `render(..., user=request.state.user)` is repeated in `post_list`, `_form` and `main.refuse`.\n- **Data Clumps**\n  - `path` and `post_id` are threaded through every `posts.*` function.\n  - `title_value=..., body_value=...` travel together in every `_form` call.\n- **Primitive Obsession / Repeated Switches**\n  - `'draft'` and `'published'` appear as bare strings in the `db.py` CHECK, in `posts.publish` and `unpublish`, and in `post_form.html` (`post.status == 'draft'`).\n  - Action strings such as `\"post.delete\"` are repeated across `RULES`, the routes and the templates, so adding an action touches three places (Shotgun Surgery).\n  - Role strings are typed as `\"admin\"` and `\"editor\"` in the tests, even though `permissions.ADMIN` and `EDITOR` exist.\n- **Feature Envy**: in the `change_slug` route, `code = 409 if isinstance(error, posts.SlugTaken) else 400` puts the error-to-status mapping in the route. A `status_code` attribute on `PostError` would avoid this.\n- **Mysterious Name**\n  - `_db()` returns a `Path`, not a database.\n  - `_form` and `post_to` (tests) are vague.\n  - `EVERYONE` means \"admin or editor\".\n  - The route `change_slug` shares a name with `posts.change_slug`.\n  - The `title` render argument (page title) sits next to the Post's `title` field, which is why `title_value` exists.\n  - `tests/test_t02_posts.py` defines its own `ADMIN_ONLY` list, which reads like `permissions.ADMIN_ONLY`.\n- **Speculative Generality / leftovers**\n  - `for path in (f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", ):` is a one-element loop in `test_editor_can_do_every_editor_action`.\n  - The `slug_form` GET route only redirects to `#slug`.\n  - `require()` raises `KeyError` for unknown actions, and `allowed()` defaults unknown actions to empty. The second guard duplicates the first.\n- **Middle Man**: `posts.unpublish` and `posts.delete` are thin one-statement wrappers. This is mild and consistent with the module style.\n- **Tests**: `time.sleep(0.01)` timing dependence appears in 3 tests. It is not a smell from the list, but it could be flaky.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>63494</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>3</tool_uses><duration_ms>29777</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

It is not a smell from the list, but it could be flaky.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>63494</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>3</tool_uses><duration_ms>29777</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"},"uuid":"b5879553-d64c-41c0-a7ea-a318883651c3","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:58:56.941Z","permissionMode":"bypassPermissions","origin":{"kind":"task-notification","producer":"session-task"},"promptSource":"system","turnOrigin":"task_notification","turnPosition":{"promptIndex":1,"turnIndex":2},"queueSkipAttachments":true,"userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The user can send it another message and resume it, so the same task-id may notify more than once.</note>\n<result>**Spec review of T02 (ticket #3), uncommitted diff on bc86dfc.** I read the diff but could not run the tests, because `python` isn't on the PATH in this shell.\n\n**(a) Missing or partial**\n- Ticket: \"Created, updated and First-published times are recorded automatically.\" Publish and unpublish don't touch `updated_at`, and no test pins what should happen. It's probably fine, since the spec ties \"Updated\" to title or Body changes, but the choice is unstated.\n- Ticket: \"Tests… cover each allowed and refused case in the Post part of the permission matrix.\" There is no test that an Editor can publish or unpublish a Post written by someone else. Only edit is tested cross-author. Story 14 says \"including another Editor's\".\n- Ticket: \"signed-out visitor is sent to sign-in.\" Tests cover this, but only for signed-out requests to routes that exist. `test_signed_out_visitors_are_sent_to_sign_in` asserts the redirect goes to `/login` for POSTs with no CSRF token. That passes only because `AdminGuard` runs before the app-level CSRF dependency. It works, but the test doesn't say why.\n- Ticket: \"An Editor who tries an Admin-only action … sees the page.\" The CSRF check runs before the permission check. An Editor's direct submission without a token gets a bare 403, not the refusal page. No test covers this ordering, and a grader could hit it.\n\n**(b) Scope creep**\n- `GET /admin/posts/{id}/slug` is a redirect route that exists only to give an Admin-only \"typed address\". The parametrized tests then enshrine it. It's defensible for \"by typed address\", but nothing in the spec asks for it.\n- `SlugTaken` returns 409 and `SlugInvalid` returns 400, with tests. The spec never mentions slug validation or collision on an Admin change. This is reasonable but unrequested.\n- The `slugify` fallback to `\"post\"` for titles like \"!!!\" has a test. The spec is silent.\n- `post_list.html`, `/admin/posts` and the dashboard link are close to the later Content list ticket. Story 48 says \"one Content list I can filter by Post or Page\". A separate Posts-only list is a different screen, so this looks like a throwaway.\n- The edit screen shows Created, Updated and Author in a `&lt;dl&gt;`. This is needed for the tests to observe those values, but it is extra UI.\n\n**(c) Looks implemented but may be wrong**\n- Spec: \"Slug edits are not shown to Editors.\" The edit page shows the slug as read-only `&lt;code id=\"post-slug\"&gt;` to Editors. The `slug_of()` helper depends on that. Read-only display isn't editing, but the tests are built on a visible slug, so check that this is the intent.\n- Spec: \"Editors never enter it.\" `test_editors_never_enter_a_slug` checks only the New Post form for the word \"slug\". The edit page for Editors shows \"Slug\" in the `&lt;dl&gt;`, so the assertion is narrower than its name.\n- `test_first_published_date_is_set_once_and_kept` uses `time.sleep(0.01)`. Timestamps have microsecond precision, so it works. It is still timing-dependent.\n- `test_admin_is_not_refused_admin_only_actions` accepts status 200 or 303 for delete and slug. That is weak. The delete test confirms it worked, but this test doesn't. It passes even if the action silently does nothing.\n- `test_editor_is_refused_every_admin_only_post_action` checks only that the slug is unchanged. It never checks that the Post still exists after the refused delete (the 404 check is missing), so a delete that went through anyway would not be caught.\n- `change_slug` runs `slugify()` on the Admin's input. This lowercases and rewrites it, so \"Afrobeat Friday\" becomes `afrobeat-friday`. It fits the test but is not in the spec.\n\n**Test misunderstanding.** The clearest case is the last weak-assertion item above, where the refused-delete test never verifies the Post survives. I found no test that contradicts the spec outright.</result>\n<usage><subagent_tokens>68399</subagent_tokens><tool_uses>5</tool_uses><duration_ms>31647</duration_ms></usage>\n</task-notification>"}

{"parentUuid":"93d2f9dc-851e-47e9-83ff-b2c0c657019c","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWywab6UBYM222mnLkbn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012EMPDTp16tkuzXnQNbsHZ5","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"gh issue comment 3 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T02 and how each was resolved\n\nReviewed as two axes (Standards, Spec) against the uncommitted diff on `bc86dfc`. Full suite: 97 passed.\n\n### Standards\nNo hard violations. CSRF on all 6 POST forms, no new dependency, no `site/` changes, and every permission check is server-side via `permissions.require()`.\n\nJudgement calls, **not changed** (would be refactors beyond the ticket):\n- Duplicated `_found(...)` + one-line call in the publish/unpublish/delete routes, and repeated `with closing(connect(path))` in `posts.py`.\n- Bare `'draft'`/`'published'` strings and repeated action-name strings across `RULES`, routes and templates.\n- Error-to-HTTP-status mapping (`409` vs `400`) lives in the `change_slug` route.\n- Naming: `_db()` returns a Path, `EVERYONE` means admin-or-editor, `author_name` vs Display name.\n- `time.sleep(0.01)` in 3 tests. Timestamps have microsecond precision, so this is a safety margin, not a dependency.\n\nJudgement calls **fixed**:\n- Removed a one-element loop in `test_editor_can_do_every_editor_action`.\n\n### Spec\nWeak tests, **fixed**:\n- Refused-delete test now also checks the Post still exists and is listed.\n- Admin-allowed test now proves the action took effect (deleted -> 404, slug changed) instead of accepting any 200/303.\n- Added: an Editor can publish and unpublish a Post written by someone else (story 14).\n- `test_editors_never_enter_a_slug` now also asserts the Editor's edit screen has no slug input.\n\nNoted, **not changed**:\n- The CSRF check runs before the permission check, so a direct submission with no token gets a bare 403 rather than the refusal page. A form submitted from the console always carries a token, and rejecting forged requests first is the safer order.\n- `updated_at` changes on title/Body edits only, not on publish/unpublish. This matches the spec's \"Updated date\" definition.\n- `GET /admin/posts/{id}/slug` (redirect to the edit page's slug form), slug 400/409 validation, the `post` fallback slug, and the Posts-only list at `/admin/posts` are small additions the spec doesn't spell out. The list is a stand-in until the T06 Content list.\n- Editors see the slug read-only; they cannot edit it.\n\nMutation checks (temporarily breaking the code): editor-may-delete (4 tests fail), first-published overwritten (2), slug regenerated on title edit (1), editor-may-change-slug (5), delete button shown to editors (1), slug numbering off (1). Each mutation was caught, then reverted.\nEOF\n)\" && git add app tests templates && git status --short","description":"Post review findings on issue #3 and stage T02 files"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1779,"cache_read_input_tokens":102097,"output_tokens":1346,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":211},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1779,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1346,"cache_read_input_tokens":102097,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1779,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1779},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_012EMPDTp16tkuzXnQNbsHZ5":{"command":"cd /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms && gh issue comment 3 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## /code-review findings for T02 and how each was resolved\n\nReviewed as two axes (Standards, Spec) against the uncommitted diff on `bc86dfc`. {"parentUuid":"8f4acca6-ed0a-4d91-b2db-e5bafa27db73","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWyxrmxUud8S9A5cZR2h","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Ticket #3 is implemented and committed as `cc4c2af` (message starts `T02:`, ends `Closes #3`). The full suite passes (97 tests). Nothing was typechecked, because the repo has no typechecker configured.\n\n**Ticket numbering:** you said `#3`, but issue #3 is titled \"T02\" and T03 is #4. I built #3, which is T02, and set `.claude/state/phase` to `T02`. If you meant T03, it's a different ticket (published Posts going Live), so tell me and I'll switch.\n\n## What was built\n- **Posts:** an Editor or Admin can write a Post, save it as a Draft, edit it, and publish or unpublish it. Author and created, updated and first-published times are recorded automatically.\n- **Slug:** it is generated once from the title, with a number added if it's taken. It never changes on edit. Only the Admin can change it, and the form warns that this can break an old link.\n- **Permissions:** all rules live in one table, `app/permissions.py`, and the server checks it before looking anything up. Only the Admin can delete a Post or change a slug.\n- **Refusal:** an Editor who tries an Admin-only action gets the \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Signed-out visitors are redirected to sign-in.\n- **Tests:** 47 new tests are in `tests/test_t02_posts.py`, at the admin HTTP seam the spec had already fixed.\n\n## Tests read before passing\nI wrote the tests first and saw 35 of 46 fail. When they went green I broke the code on purpose in six ways, for example letting an Editor delete or regenerating the slug on a title edit. The tests caught every one. A slip while restoring files briefly corrupted `app/posts.py`; I rewrote it and re-ran the mutations with safe backups, and the final files match the intended ones.\n\n## Code review\n- **Standards:** no hard violations, only refactor-style smells (duplicated helper calls, repeated status strings). I left those alone, as they're beyond this ticket.\n- **Spec:** it flagged a few weak tests, which I strengthened. For example, the refused-delete test now checks the Post still exists.\n- The findings and how each was resolved are posted on the issue as the project rule requires: https://github.com/yawoppongkrampah-hub/iphs400-mp2-cms/issues/3#issuecomment-5882788945\n\n## Things to know\n- A direct submission with no CSRF token gets a plain 403 rather than the refusal page, because the CSRF check runs first. Any form from the console carries a token, so I left it.\n- I added a basic Posts-only list at `/admin/posts` so the new Posts are reachable. It's a stand-in until the T06 Content list.\n- I didn't commit `notes/usage-ledger.csv` or the session-08 transcript. Both were already dirty before this session and aren't part of the ticket.\n- I didn't check `/status` for a non-Anthropic backend, so there's no `Backend:` trailer on the commit.\n- Nothing is pushed, and issue #3 is still open. `Closes #3` will close it when the commit reaches GitHub."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":487,"cache_read_input_tokens":105477,"output_tokens":1047,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":487,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1047,"cache_read_input_tokens":105477,"cache_creation_input_tokens":487,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":487},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWyxrHSd4P1pbShbkTRr","type":"assistant","uuid":"be85dff3-e0a7-425e-a541-3c0abd7354f8","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:59:47.587Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a34708fe-9b19-4e3d-8c71-dc5a652121bc","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
